'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { getAnimeById, AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Star, Calendar, PlayCircle, Plus, Share2, Image as ImageIcon, Loader2, TrendingUp, Users, Clock } from 'lucide-react'
import { AddToCollectionDialog } from '@/components/anime/AddToCollectionDialog'
import { ShareAnimeDialog } from '@/components/anime/ShareAnimeDialog'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { translateGenres } from '@/lib/genres'

export default function AnimePage() {
  const params = useParams()
  const animeId = parseInt(params.id as string)
  const [anime, setAnime] = useState<AniListAnime | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [isAddDialogOpen, setIsAddDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [isShareDialogOpen, setIsShareDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const loadAnime = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        const data = await getAnimeById(animeId)
        setAnime(data)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить информацию об аниме. Включите VPN.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnime()
  }, [animeId, mounted])

  const getStatusColor = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'RELEASING':
        return 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border-green-500/30'
      case 'FINISHED':
        return 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30'
      case 'NOT_YET_RELEASED':
        return 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 border-purple-500/30'
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border-red-500/30'
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400 border-gray-500/30'
    }
  }

  const getStatusText = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'RELEASING':
        return 'Выходит'
      case 'FINISHED':
        return 'Завершено'
      case 'NOT_YET_RELEASED':
        return 'Анонс'
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return 'Отменено'
      default:
        return status
    }
  }

  const getTypeText = (type: string): string => {
    const typeMap: Record<string, string> = {
      'TV': 'ТВ',
      'TV_SHORT': 'ТВ (короткий)',
      'MOVIE': 'Фильм',
      'OVA': 'OVA',
      'ONA': 'ONA',
      'SPECIAL': 'Спец.',
      'MUSIC': 'Музыка',
    }
    return typeMap[type] || type
  }

  const getScore = (): number | null => {
    return anime?.averageScore ? anime.averageScore / 10 : null
  }

  const title = anime?.title.english || anime?.title.romaji || 'Без названия'

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-purple-500" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <div className="relative">
          <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-purple-500" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-purple-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error || !anime) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 mb-4">
            <span className="text-4xl">⚠️</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-red-400 text-lg mb-4">{error || 'Не удалось загрузить аниме'}</p>
          <Link href="/">
            <Button className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-pink-600 hover:from-purple-700 hover:to-pink-700 text-white border-0">
              Вернуться на главную
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
      {/* Banner */}
      {anime.bannerImage && !imageError && (
        <div className="relative h-80 md:h-96 overflow-hidden">
          <img
            src={anime.bannerImage}
            alt={title}
            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
            onError={() => setImageError(true)}
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-gray-900 via-gray-900/80 to-transparent" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-900 via-gray-900/50 to-transparent" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Content */}
      <div className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8 -mt-48 relative z-10">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {/* Left Column - Cover */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-1">
            <Card className="overflow-hidden bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/20 shadow-2xl shadow-purple-500/10">
              {!imageError && anime.coverImage?.large ? (
                <img
                  src={anime.coverImage.large}
                  alt={title}
                  className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover"
                  onError={() => setImageError(true)}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full aspect-[2/3] bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-900/50 to-pink-900/50 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <ImageIcon className="h-24 w-24 text-purple-400/50" />
                </div>
              )}
              <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex gap-3">
                  <Button
                    className="flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-pink-600 hover:from-purple-700 hover:to-pink-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-purple-500/30"
                    onClick={() => setIsAddDialogOpen(true)}
                  >
                    <Plus className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                    В коллекцию
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    className="border-purple-500/30 hover:bg-purple-600/20"
                    onClick={() => setIsShareDialogOpen(true)}
                  >
                    <Share2 className="h-5 w-5" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column - Info */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
            <div>
              <Link href="/" className="text-sm text-purple-300/70 hover:text-purple-400 transition-colors mb-4 inline-block">
                ← Вернуться на главную
              </Link>
              <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold mb-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                {title}
              </h1>
              {anime.title.native && (
                <p className="text-xl text-purple-300/80 mb-2">{anime.title.native}</p>
              )}
              {anime.title.romaji && anime.title.romaji !== anime.title.english && (
                <p className="text-sm text-purple-300/60">{anime.title.romaji}</p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Badges */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <Badge className={`${getStatusColor(anime.status)} border backdrop-blur-md shadow-lg`}>
                {getStatusText(anime.status)}
              </Badge>
              <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-purple-900/30 text-purple-300 border-purple-500/30">
                {getTypeText(anime.type)}
              </Badge>
              {anime.season && anime.seasonYear && (
                <Badge variant="outline" className="border-purple-500/30 text-purple-300">
                  {anime.season} {anime.seasonYear}
                </Badge>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Stats */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {getScore() && (
                <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/20 hover:border-purple-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                      <Star className="h-6 w-6 fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" />
                      <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{getScore()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-purple-300/70">Оценка</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
              {anime.episodes && (
                <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/20 hover:border-purple-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                      <PlayCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-purple-400" />
                      <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{anime.episodes}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-purple-300/70">Эпизодов</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
              {anime.seasonYear && (
                <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/20 hover:border-purple-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                      <Calendar className="h-6 w-6 text-purple-400" />
                      <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{anime.seasonYear}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-purple-300/70">Год</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
              <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/20 hover:border-purple-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                    <TrendingUp className="h-6 w-6 text-purple-400" />
                    <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{anime.averageScore}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-purple-300/70">Рейтинг</p>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </div>

            {/* Genres */}
            {anime.genres && anime.genres.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Users className="h-5 w-5 text-purple-400" />
                  Жанры
                </h3>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {translateGenres(anime.genres).map((genre, index) => (
                    <Link key={index} href={`/genre/${encodeURIComponent(anime.genres![index])}`}>
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="hover:bg-purple-600 hover:text-white transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer bg-purple-900/30 text-purple-300 border-purple-500/30 px-3 py-1.5">
                        {genre}
                      </Badge>
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Description */}
            {anime.description && (
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Clock className="h-5 w-5 text-purple-400" />
                  Описание
                </h3>
                <div
                  className="text-purple-200/80 leading-relaxed text-lg"
                  dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: anime.description }}
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Dialogs */}
      <Dialog open={isAddDialogOpen} onOpenChange={setIsAddDialogOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-gray-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/20">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-white">Добавить в коллекцию</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-purple-300/70">
              {title}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <AddToCollectionDialog
            animeId={anime.id}
            animeTitle={title}
            onClose={() => setIsAddDialogOpen(false)}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <ShareAnimeDialog
        animeId={anime.id}
        animeTitle={title}
        isOpen={isShareDialogOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsShareDialogOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { AniListAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/AniListAnimeCard'
import { AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { getAnimeByGenre } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft, Filter } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'

export default function GenrePage() {
  const params = useParams()
  const genre = decodeURIComponent(params.genre as string)
  const [animeList, setAnimeList] = useState<AniListAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const loadAnime = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await getAnimeByGenre(genre)
        setAnimeList(data.Page.media)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime by genre:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме по жанру. Включите VPN.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnime()
  }, [genre, mounted])

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-purple-500" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
      {/* Header */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-16 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600/30 via-pink-600/20 to-purple-600/30 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjEpIi8+PC9zdmc+')] opacity-30" />
        <div className="container mx-auto relative z-10">
          <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-purple-300/70 hover:text-purple-400 transition-colors mb-6">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Вернуться на главную
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-600 to-pink-600 shadow-lg shadow-purple-500/30">
              <Filter className="h-8 w-8 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent mb-2">
                {translateGenre(genre)}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-purple-200/80 text-lg">
                Аниме в жанре {translateGenre(genre)}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Anime List */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="relative">
                <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-purple-500" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-purple-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : error ? (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">⚠️</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-red-400 text-lg mb-4">{error}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-purple-300/70">
                Включите VPN для доступа к AniList API
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : animeList.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">🔍</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-purple-300/70 text-lg">
                Нет аниме в этом жанре
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6">
              {animeList.map((anime) => (
                <AniListAnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { AniListAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/AniListAnimeCard'
import { AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { searchAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Search, Loader2, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SearchPage() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [results, setResults] = useState<AniListAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [debouncedQuery, setDebouncedQuery] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  // Debounce search
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setDebouncedQuery(query)
    }, 500)

    return () => clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [query, mounted])

  // Search when debounced query changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted || !debouncedQuery.trim() || debouncedQuery.length < 2) {
      setResults([])
      return
    }

    const performSearch = async () => {
      setLoading(true)
      setError('')

      try {
        const response = await searchAnime(debouncedQuery)
        setResults(response.Page.media)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Search error:', err)
        setError('Не удалось выполнить поиск. Включите VPN.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    performSearch()
  }, [debouncedQuery, mounted])

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <div className="relative">
          <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-purple-500" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-purple-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-purple-900/20 to-gray-900">
      {/* Header */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-16 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600/30 via-pink-600/20 to-purple-600/30 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjEpIi8+PC9zdmc+')] opacity-30" />
        <div className="container mx-auto text-center relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-purple-400 animate-pulse" />
            <h1 className="text-5xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Поиск аниме
            </h1>
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-pink-400 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xl text-purple-200/80 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Найдите аниме и добавьте в свою коллекцию
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Search */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="flex-1 relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-5 w-5 text-purple-400" />
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Введите название аниме..."
                  value={query}
                  onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="pl-12 bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-purple-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-purple-300/50 focus:border-purple-500/50 focus:ring-purple-500/20 h-14 text-lg"
                />
              </div>
              <Button
                disabled={loading || !query.trim()}
                onClick={() => setDebouncedQuery(query)}
                className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-pink-600 hover:from-purple-700 hover:to-pink-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-purple-500/30 h-14 px-8"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
                    Поиск...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Search className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                    Найти
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
              <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-6 py-4 rounded-xl text-center">
                {error}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {loading && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="relative">
                <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-purple-500" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-purple-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6">
              {results.map((anime) => (
                <AniListAnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length === 0 && query && query.length >= 2 && (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">🔍</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-purple-300/70 text-lg">
                Ничего не найдено. Попробуйте другой запрос.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length === 0 && !query && (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 mb-4">
                <Search className="h-10 w-10 text-purple-400" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-purple-300/70 text-lg">
                Введите название аниме для поиска
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Filter, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { getAllGenres } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'

interface GenreFilterDialogProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  selectedGenre: string
  onGenreSelect: (genre: string) => void
}

export function GenreFilterDialog({ isOpen, onClose, selectedGenre, onGenreSelect }: GenreFilterDialogProps) {
  const [genres, setGenres] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return

    const loadGenres = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        const genreList = await getAllGenres()
        setGenres(genreList)
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error loading genres:', err)
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadGenres()
  }, [isOpen])

  return (
    <>
      <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-gray-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 max-w-2xl max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Filter className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
              Фильтр по жанрам
            </DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-blue-300/70">
              Выберите жанр для фильтрации аниме
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-blue-500" />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 py-4">
              {genres.map((genre) => (
                <Badge
                  key={genre}
                  variant={selectedGenre === genre ? 'default' : 'secondary'}
                  className={`cursor-pointer transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105 ${
                    selectedGenre === genre
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30'
                      : 'bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/50'
                  }`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onGenreSelect(selectedGenre === genre ? '' : genre)
                    onClose()
                  }}
                >
                  {translateGenre(genre)}
                </Badge>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {selectedGenre && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-blue-500/20">
              <span className="text-sm text-blue-300">
                Выбран: <span className="text-white font-medium">{translateGenre(selectedGenre)}</span>
              </span>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => {
                  onGenreSelect('')
                  onClose()
                }}
                className="border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white"
              >
                <X className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Сбросить
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Star, Calendar, PlayCircle, Plus, Share2, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { AddToCollectionDialog } from './AddToCollectionDialog'
import { ShareAnimeDialog } from './ShareAnimeDialog'
import Link from 'next/link'

interface AniListAnimeCardProps {
  anime: AniListAnime
}

export function AniListAnimeCard({ anime }: AniListAnimeCardProps) {
  const [isAddDialogOpen, setIsAddDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [isShareDialogOpen, setIsShareDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false)

  const getStatusColor = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'RELEASING':
        return 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border-green-500/30'
      case 'FINISHED':
        return 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30'
      case 'NOT_YET_RELEASED':
        return 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400 border-cyan-500/30'
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border-red-500/30'
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400 border-gray-500/30'
    }
  }

  const getStatusText = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'RELEASING':
        return 'Выходит'
      case 'FINISHED':
        return 'Завершено'
      case 'NOT_YET_RELEASED':
        return 'Анонс'
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return 'Отменено'
      default:
        return status
    }
  }

  const getTypeText = (type: string): string => {
    const typeMap: Record<string, string> = {
      'TV': 'ТВ',
      'TV_SHORT': 'ТВ (короткий)',
      'MOVIE': 'Фильм',
      'OVA': 'OVA',
      'ONA': 'ONA',
      'SPECIAL': 'Спец.',
      'MUSIC': 'Музыка',
    }
    return typeMap[type] || type
  }

  const getScore = (): number | null => {
    return anime.averageScore ? anime.averageScore / 10 : null
  }

  const title = anime.title.english || anime.title.romaji || 'Без названия'

  return (
    <>
      <Card className="overflow-hidden hover:shadow-2xl transition-all duration-500 hover:scale-105 bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900/80 to-blue-900/30 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 hover:border-blue-500/50 group">
        <Link href={`/anime/${anime.id}`}>
          <div className="relative overflow-hidden">
            {imageError || !anime.coverImage?.large ? (
              <div className="w-full aspect-[2/3] bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-900/50 to-cyan-900/50 flex items-center justify-center">
                <div className="text-center p-4">
                  <ImageIcon className="h-16 w-16 mx-auto mb-3 text-blue-400/50" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-300/70">{title}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <img
                src={anime.coverImage.large}
                alt={title}
                className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-110"
                loading="lazy"
                onError={(e) => {
                  console.error('Image load error:', anime.coverImage.large)
                  setImageError(true)
                }}
              />
            )}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 via-black/20 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300" />

            {getScore() && (
              <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-blue-500/30 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/20">
                <Star className="h-4 w-4 fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" />
                <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm">{getScore()}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="absolute top-3 left-3">
              <Badge className={`${getStatusColor(anime.status)} border backdrop-blur-md shadow-lg`}>
                {getStatusText(anime.status)}
              </Badge>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Link>

        <CardHeader className="pb-3 pt-4">
          <Link href={`/anime/${anime.id}`}>
            <CardTitle className="line-clamp-2 hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer text-lg font-bold">
              {title}
            </CardTitle>
          </Link>
          {anime.title.native && (
            <CardDescription className="line-clamp-1 text-xs text-blue-300/70">
              {anime.title.native}
            </CardDescription>
          )}
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {anime.genres?.slice(0, 3).map((genre, index) => (
              <Link key={index} href={`/genre/${encodeURIComponent(genre)}`}>
                <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs hover:bg-blue-600 hover:text-white transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30">
                  {genre}
                </Badge>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-blue-200/70">
            {anime.episodes && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <PlayCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{anime.episodes} эп.</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {anime.seasonYear && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{anime.seasonYear}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <Button
              size="sm"
              className="flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-cyan-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30 hover:shadow-blue-500/50 transition-all duration-300"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault()
                setIsAddDialogOpen(true)
              }}
            >
              <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
              В коллекцию
            </Button>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              className="border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 hover:border-blue-500/50 transition-all duration-300"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault()
                setIsShareDialogOpen(true)
              }}
            >
              <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Dialog open={isAddDialogOpen} onOpenChange={setIsAddDialogOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-gray-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-white">Добавить в коллекцию</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-blue-300/70">
              {title}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <AddToCollectionDialog
            animeId={anime.id}
            animeTitle={title}
            onClose={() => setIsAddDialogOpen(false)}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <ShareAnimeDialog
        animeId={anime.id}
        animeTitle={title}
        isOpen={isShareDialogOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsShareDialogOpen(false)}
      />
    </>
  )
}
Теперь обновлю диалог добавления в коллекцию:

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select'
import { Star, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

interface AddToCollectionDialogProps {
  animeId: number
  animeTitle: string
  onClose: () => void
}

export function AddToCollectionDialog({ animeId, animeTitle, onClose }: AddToCollectionDialogProps) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'watching' | 'completed' | 'plan_to_watch' | 'dropped'>('watching')
  const [rating, setRating] = useState<number>(0)
  const [review, setReview] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')

    try {
      const supabase = createClient()
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

      if (!user) {
        throw new Error('Вы не авторизованы')
      }

      const { error } = await supabase.from('anime_collection').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        anime_id: animeId,
        status,
        rating: rating > 0 ? rating : null,
        review: review.trim() || null,
      })

      if (error) throw error

      onClose()
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Ошибка при добавлении')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
      {error && (
        <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="status" className="text-blue-300">Статус</Label>
        <Select value={status} onValueChange={(value: any) => setStatus(value)}>
          <SelectTrigger className="bg-gray-800/50 border-blue-500/30 text-white">
            <SelectValue placeholder="Выберите статус" />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent className="bg-gray-900/95 border-blue-500/30">
            <SelectItem value="watching">👀 Смотрю</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="completed">✅ Просмотрено</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="plan_to_watch">📋 В планах</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="dropped">❌ Брошено</SelectItem>
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="rating" className="text-blue-300">Оценка (1-100)</Label>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Star className="h-5 w-5 text-yellow-400" />
          <Input
            id="rating"
            type="number"
            min="1"
            max="100"
            placeholder="Оцените от 1 до 100"
            value={rating || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setRating(parseInt(e.target.value) || 0)}
            className="bg-gray-800/50 border-blue-500/30 text-white"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="review" className="text-blue-300">Отзыв</Label>
        <Textarea
          id="review"
          placeholder="Напишите ваш отзыв..."
          value={review}
          onChange={(e) => setReview(e.target.value)}
          rows={4}
          className="bg-gray-800/50 border-blue-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-blue-300/50"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          onClick={onClose}
          disabled={loading}
          className="flex-1 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white"
        >
          Отмена
        </Button>
        <Button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-cyan-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30"
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
              Добавление...
            </>
          ) : (
            'Добавить'
          )}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { AniListAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/AniListAnimeCard'
import { AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { getAiringAnime, getUpcomingAnime, getCompletedAnime, getMovies, getLatestAnime, getAnimeByGenre } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs'
import { Loader2, Search, Filter, Sparkles, TrendingUp, Clock, Calendar, Film, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { SakuraAnimation } from '@/components/effects/SakuraAnimation'

import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'

type TabType = 'latest' | 'airing' | 'upcoming' | 'completed' | 'movies'

export default function HomePage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('latest')
  const [animeList, setAnimeList] = useState<AniListAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [selectedGenre, setSelectedGenre] = useState<string>('')
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('')
  const [isGenreDialogOpen, setIsGenreDialogOpen] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const loadAnime = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        let data
        switch (activeTab) {
          case 'airing':
            data = await getAiringAnime()
            break
          case 'upcoming':
            data = await getUpcomingAnime()
            break
          case 'completed':
            data = await getCompletedAnime()
            break
          case 'movies':
            data = await getMovies()
            break
          case 'latest':
          default:
            data = await getLatestAnime()
            break
        }

        setAnimeList(data.Page.media)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме. Убедитесь, что VPN включен.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnime()
  }, [activeTab, mounted])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted || !selectedGenre) return

    const loadAnimeByGenre = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await getAnimeByGenre(selectedGenre)
        setAnimeList(data.Page.media)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime by genre:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме по жанру.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnimeByGenre()
  }, [selectedGenre, mounted])

  const filteredAnime = animeList.filter(anime => {
    if (!searchQuery) return true
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase()
    const title = (anime.title.english || anime.title.romaji || '').toLowerCase()
    const nativeTitle = (anime.title.native || '').toLowerCase()
    return title.includes(query) || nativeTitle.includes(query)
  })

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/30 to-gray-900">
      <SakuraAnimation />

      {/* Hero Section */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-20 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600/30 via-cyan-600/20 to-blue-600/30 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjEpIi8+PC9zdmc+')] opacity-30" />
        <div className="container mx-auto text-center relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-blue-400 animate-pulse" />
            <h1 className="text-6xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 via-cyan-400 to-blue-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent animate-gradient-x">
              AnimeTracker
            </h1>
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-cyan-400 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xl text-blue-200/80 mb-8 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Отслеживайте своё аниме, находите друзей и общайтесь — всё в одном месте
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Search and Filters */}
      <section className="px-4 mb-8">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <div className="flex gap-4 mb-6">
            <div className="flex-1 relative">
              <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
              <Input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Поиск аниме..."
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                className="pl-12 bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-blue-300/50 focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-blue-500/20 h-12 text-lg"
              />
            </div>
            <Button
              onClick={() => setIsGenreDialogOpen(true)}
              className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-cyan-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30 h-12 px-6"
            >
              <Filter className="h-5 w-5 mr-2" />
              Жанры
            </Button>
            {selectedGenre && (
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => setSelectedGenre('')}
                className="border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white h-12 px-6"
              >
                <span className="mr-2">{translateGenre(selectedGenre)}</span>
                ✕
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Anime Tabs */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <Tabs value={activeTab} onValueChange={(v) => {
            setActiveTab(v as TabType)
            setSelectedGenre('')
          }} className="w-full">
            <TabsList className="grid w-full grid-cols-5 mb-8 bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 h-16">
              <TabsTrigger value="latest" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Последние
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="airing" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <Clock className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Онгоинги
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="upcoming" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <Calendar className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Анонсы
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="completed" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Завершенные

                Фильмы

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { getAnimeById, AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Star, Calendar, PlayCircle, Plus, Share2, Image as ImageIcon, Loader2, TrendingUp, Users, Clock } from 'lucide-react'
import { AddToCollectionDialog } from '@/components/anime/AddToCollectionDialog'
import { ShareAnimeDialog } from '@/components/anime/ShareAnimeDialog'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { translateGenres } from '@/lib/genres'

export default function AnimePage() {
  const params = useParams()
  const animeId = parseInt(params.id as string)
  const [anime, setAnime] = useState<AniListAnime | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [isAddDialogOpen, setIsAddDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [isShareDialogOpen, setIsShareDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const loadAnime = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        const data = await getAnimeById(animeId)
        setAnime(data)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить информацию об аниме. Включите VPN.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnime()
  }, [animeId, mounted])

  const getStatusColor = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'RELEASING':
        return 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border-green-500/30'
      case 'FINISHED':
        return 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30'
      case 'NOT_YET_RELEASED':
        return 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400 border-cyan-500/30'
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border-red-500/30'
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400 border-gray-500/30'
    }
  }

  const getStatusText = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'RELEASING':
        return 'Выходит'
      case 'FINISHED':
        return 'Завершено'
      case 'NOT_YET_RELEASED':
        return 'Анонс'
      case 'CANCELLED':
        return 'Отменено'
      default:
        return status
    }
  }

  const getTypeText = (type: string): string => {
    const typeMap: Record<string, string> = {
      'TV': 'ТВ',
      'TV_SHORT': 'ТВ (короткий)',
      'MOVIE': 'Фильм',
      'OVA': 'OVA',
      'ONA': 'ONA',
      'SPECIAL': 'Спец.',
      'MUSIC': 'Музыка',
    }
    return typeMap[type] || type
  }

  const getScore = (): number | null => {
    return anime?.averageScore ? anime.averageScore / 10 : null
  }

  const title = anime?.title.english || anime?.title.romaji || 'Без названия'

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <div className="relative">
          <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error || !anime) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 mb-4">
            <span className="text-4xl">⚠️</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-red-400 text-lg mb-4">{error || 'Не удалось загрузить аниме'}</p>
          <Link href="/">
            <Button className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-cyan-700 text-white border-0">
              Вернуться на главную
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
      {/* Banner */}
      {anime.bannerImage && !imageError && (
        <div className="relative h-80 md:h-96 overflow-hidden">
          <img
            src={anime.bannerImage}
            alt={title}
            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
            onError={() => setImageError(true)}
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-gray-900 via-gray-900/80 to-transparent" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-900 via-gray-900/50 to-transparent" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Content */}
      <div className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8 -mt-48 relative z-10">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {/* Left Column - Cover */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-1">
            <Card className="overflow-hidden bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 shadow-2xl shadow-blue-500/10">
              {!imageError && anime.coverImage?.large ? (
                <img
                  src={anime.coverImage.large}
                  alt={title}
                  className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover"
                  onError={() => setImageError(true)}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full aspect-[2/3] bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-900/50 to-cyan-900/50 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <ImageIcon className="h-24 w-24 text-blue-400/50" />
                </div>
              )}
              <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex gap-3">
                  <Button
                    className="flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-cyan-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30"
                    onClick={() => setIsAddDialogOpen(true)}
                  >
                    <Plus className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                    В коллекцию
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    className="border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20"
                    onClick={() => setIsShareDialogOpen(true)}
                  >
                    <Share2 className="h-5 w-5" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column - Info */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
            <div>
              <Link href="/" className="text-sm text-blue-300/70 hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors mb-4 inline-block">
                ← Вернуться на главную
              </Link>
              <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold mb-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-cyan-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                {title}
              </h1>
              {anime.title.native && (
                <p className="text-xl text-blue-300/80 mb-2">{anime.title.native}</p>
              )}
              {anime.title.romaji && anime.title.romaji !== anime.title.english && (
                <p className="text-sm text-blue-300/60">{anime.title.romaji}</p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Badges */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <Badge className={`${getStatusColor(anime.status)} border backdrop-blur-md shadow-lg`}>
                {getStatusText(anime.status)}
              </Badge>
              <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30">
                {getTypeText(anime.type)}
              </Badge>
              {anime.season && anime.seasonYear && (
                <Badge variant="outline" className="border-blue-500/30 text-blue-300">
                  {anime.season} {anime.seasonYear}
                </Badge>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Stats */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {getScore() && (
                <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 hover:border-blue-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                      <Star className="h-6 w-6 fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" />
                      <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{getScore()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-blue-300/70">Оценка</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
              {anime.episodes && (
                <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 hover:border-blue-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                      <PlayCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-blue-400" />
                      <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{anime.episodes}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-blue-300/70">Эпизодов</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
              {anime.seasonYear && (
                <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 hover:border-blue-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                  <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                      <Calendar className="h-6 w-6 text-blue-400" />
                      <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{anime.seasonYear}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-blue-300/70">Год</p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
              <Card className="bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 hover:border-blue-500/40 transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105">
                <CardContent className="p-4 text-center">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
                    <TrendingUp className="h-6 w-6 text-blue-400" />
                    <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">{anime.averageScore}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-blue-300/70">Рейтинг</p>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </div>

            {/* Genres */}
            {anime.genres && anime.genres.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Users className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
                  Жанры
                </h3>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {translateGenres(anime.genres).map((genre, index) => (
                    <Link key={index} href={`/genre/${encodeURIComponent(anime.genres![index])}`}>
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="hover:bg-blue-600 hover:text-white transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30 px-3 py-1.5">
                        {genre}
                      </Badge>
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Description */}
            {anime.description && (
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Clock className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
                  Описание
                </h3>
                <div
                  className="text-blue-200/80 leading-relaxed text-lg"
                  dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: anime.description }}
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Dialogs */}
      <Dialog open={isAddDialogOpen} onOpenChange={setIsAddDialogOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-gray-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-white">Добавить в коллекцию</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-blue-300/70">
              {title}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <AddToCollectionDialog
            animeId={anime.id}
            animeTitle={title}
            onClose={() => setIsAddDialogOpen(false)}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <ShareAnimeDialog
        animeId={anime.id}
        animeTitle={title}
        isOpen={isShareDialogOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsShareDialogOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { AniListAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/AniListAnimeCard'
import { AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { getAnimeByGenre } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft, Filter } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'

export default function GenrePage() {
  const params = useParams()
  const genre = decodeURIComponent(params.genre as string)
  const [animeList, setAnimeList] = useState<AniListAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const loadAnime = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await getAnimeByGenre(genre)
        setAnimeList(data.Page.media)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime by genre:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме по жанру. Включите VPN.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnime()
  }, [genre, mounted])

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
      {/* Header */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-16 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600/30 via-cyan-600/20 to-blue-600/30 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjEpIi8+PC9zdmc+')] opacity-30" />
        <div className="container mx-auto relative z-10">
          <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-blue-300/70 hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors mb-6">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Вернуться на главную
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30">
              <Filter className="h-8 w-8 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-cyan-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent mb-2">
                {translateGenre(genre)}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-blue-200/80 text-lg">
                Аниме в жанре {translateGenre(genre)}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Anime List */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="relative">
                <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : error ? (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">⚠️</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-red-400 text-lg mb-4">{error}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-blue-300/70">
                Включите VPN для доступа к AniList API
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : animeList.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">🔍</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-blue-300/70 text-lg">
                Нет аниме в этом жанре
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6">
              {animeList.map((anime) => (
                <AniListAnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
              ))}
            </div>

      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { AniListAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/AniListAnimeCard'
import { AniListAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { searchAnime } from '@/lib/anilist/client'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Search, Loader2, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SearchPage() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [results, setResults] = useState<AniListAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [debouncedQuery, setDebouncedQuery] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  // Debounce search
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setDebouncedQuery(query)
    }, 500)

    return () => clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [query, mounted])

  // Search when debounced query changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted || !debouncedQuery.trim() || debouncedQuery.length < 2) {
      setResults([])
      return
    }

    const performSearch = async () => {
      setLoading(true)
      setError('')

      try {
        const response = await searchAnime(debouncedQuery)
        setResults(response.Page.media)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Search error:', err)
        setError('Не удалось выполнить поиск. Включите VPN.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    performSearch()
  }, [debouncedQuery, mounted])

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
        <div className="relative">
          <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900 via-blue-900/20 to-gray-900">
      {/* Header */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-16 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600/30 via-cyan-600/20 to-blue-600/30 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjEpIi8+PC9zdmc+')] opacity-30" />
        <div className="container mx-auto text-center relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-blue-400 animate-pulse" />
            <h1 className="text-5xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-cyan-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Поиск аниме
            </h1>
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-cyan-400 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xl text-blue-200/80 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Найдите аниме и добавьте в свою коллекцию
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Search */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="flex-1 relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Введите название аниме..."
                  value={query}
                  onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="pl-12 bg-gray-800/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-blue-300/50 focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-blue-500/20 h-14 text-lg"
                />
              </div>
              <Button
                disabled={loading || !query.trim()}
                onClick={() => setDebouncedQuery(query)}
                className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-cyan-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30 h-14 px-8"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
                    Поиск...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Search className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                    Найти
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
              <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-6 py-4 rounded-xl text-center">
                {error}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {loading && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="relative">
                <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-xl animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6">
              {results.map((anime) => (
                <AniListAnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length === 0 && query && query.length >= 2 && (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">🔍</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-blue-300/70 text-lg">
                Ничего не найдено. Попробуйте другой запрос.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length === 0 && !query && (
            <div className="text-center py-20">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 mb-4">
                <Search className="h-10 w-10 text-blue-400" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-blue-300/70 text-lg">
                Введите название аниме для поиска
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

Write c:\Users\denis\Desktop\Claude\src\components\anime\ShareAnimeDialog.tsx

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Share2, Copy, Check, MessageCircle, Globe } from 'lucide-react'

interface ShareAnimeDialogProps {
  animeId: number
  animeTitle: string
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
}

export function ShareAnimeDialog({ animeId, animeTitle, isOpen, onClose }: ShareAnimeDialogProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/anime/${animeId}`
  const shareText = `Посмотри это аниме: ${animeTitle}`

  const copyToClipboard = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl)
      setCopied(true)
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to copy:', error)
    }
  }

  const shareOnTwitter = () => {
    const url = `https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${encodeURIComponent(shareText)}&url=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }

  const shareOnFacebook = () => {
    const url = `https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }

  const shareNative = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title: animeTitle,
          text: shareText,
          url: shareUrl,
        })
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error sharing:', error)
      }
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-gray-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 max-w-md">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-white">Поделиться аниме</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-blue-300/70">
              Поделись ссылкой на {animeTitle} с друзьями
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={shareUrl}
                  readOnly
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-gray-800/50 border border-blue-500/30 text-white rounded-md"
                />
              </div>
              <Button onClick={copyToClipboard} size="icon" variant="outline" className="border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20">
                {copied ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />}
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <Button onClick={shareNative} variant="outline" className="gap-2 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white">
                <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                Поделиться
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={shareOnTwitter} variant="outline" className="gap-2 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white">
                <MessageCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
                Twitter
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={shareOnFacebook} variant="outline" className="gap-2 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white">
                <Globe className="h-4 w-4" />
                Facebook
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={copyToClipboard} variant="outline" className="gap-2 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-white">
                {copied ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />}
                {copied ? 'Скопировано!' : 'Копировать'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  )
}
Теперь оптимизирую анимацию сакуры:

Write c:\Users\denis\Desktop\Claude\src\components\effects\SakuraAnimation.tsx
