'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'

export function SakuraAnimation() {
  const animationFrameRef = useRef<number>()
  const petalsRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement[]>([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const createPetal = () => {
      const petal = document.createElement('div')
      petal.className = 'sakura-petal'

      // Random properties
      const size = Math.random() * 8 + 4
      const startX = Math.random() * window.innerWidth
      const duration = Math.random() * 8 + 6
      const rotation = Math.random() * 360

      petal.style.cssText = `
        position: fixed;
        top: -20px;
        left: ${startX}px;
        width: ${size}px;
        height: ${size}px;
        background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255, 182, 197, 0.8) 0%, rgba(255, 105, 180, 0.6) 100%);
        border-radius: 50% 0 50% 50%;
        opacity: ${Math.random() * 0.4 + 0.2};
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 9999;
        transform: rotate(${rotation}deg);
        will-change: transform, top, left;
      `

      document.body.appendChild(petal)
      petalsRef.current.push(petal)

      // Animate petal
      const startTime = performance.now()
      const animate = (currentTime: number) => {
        const elapsed = (currentTime - startTime) / 1000
        const progress = elapsed / duration

        if (progress >= 1) {
          petal.remove()
          const index = petalsRef.current.indexOf(petal)
          if (index > -1) {
            petalsRef.current.splice(index, 1)
          }
          return
        }

        const y = progress * (window.innerHeight + 40)
        const x = startX + Math.sin(progress * Math.PI * 2) * 50
        const rot = rotation + progress * 720

        petal.style.transform = `translate(${x - startX}px, ${y}px) rotate(${rot}deg)`

        animationFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate)
      }

      animationFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate)
    }

    // Create petals periodically (less frequent for performance)
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      // Limit total petals for performance
      if (petalsRef.current.length < 20) {
        createPetal()
      }
    }, 800)

    // Add CSS
    const style = document.createElement('style')
    style.textContent = `
      .sakura-petal {
        box-shadow: 0 0 3px rgba(255, 182, 193, 0.2);
      }
    `
    document.head.appendChild(style)

    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval)
      style.remove()
      // Remove all petals
      petalsRef.current.forEach(petal => petal.remove())
      petalsRef.current = []
      if (animationFrameRef.current) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameRef.current)
      }
    }
  }, [])

  return null
}

import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface GlassCardProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  children: React.ReactNode
  className?: string
  variant?: 'default' | 'dark' | 'light'
}

export function GlassCard({ children, className, variant = 'default', ...props }: GlassCardProps) {
  const variants = {
    default: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-900/20 via-cyan-900/10 to-blue-900/20 backdrop-blur-xl border border-blue-500/20',
    dark: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900/40 via-blue-900/30 to-gray-900/40 backdrop-blur-xl border border-blue-500/30',
    light: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500/10 via-cyan-500/5 to-blue-500/10 backdrop-blur-xl border border-blue-400/30',
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-blue-500/10 hover:shadow-blue-500/20 transition-all duration-500',
        variants[variant],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

interface GlassPanelProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  children: React.ReactNode
  className?: string
  variant?: 'default' | 'dark' | 'light'
}

export function GlassPanel({ children, className, variant = 'default', ...props }: GlassPanelProps) {
  const variants = {
    default: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-900/30 via-cyan-900/20 to-blue-900/30 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-blue-500/30',
    dark: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-900/50 via-blue-900/40 to-gray-900/50 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-blue-500/40',
    light: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500/20 via-cyan-500/10 to-blue-500/20 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-blue-400/40',
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'rounded-3xl shadow-2xl shadow-blue-500/20 hover:shadow-blue-500/30 transition-all duration-500',
        variants[variant],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

interface GlassButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  children: React.ReactNode
  className?: string
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger'
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg'
}

export function GlassButton({ children, className, variant = 'primary', size = 'md', ...props }: GlassButtonProps) {
  const variants = {
    primary: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 via-cyan-600 to-blue-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:via-cyan-700 hover:to-blue-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30 hover:shadow-blue-500/50 hover:scale-105',
    secondary: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-700 via-blue-700 to-gray-700 hover:from-gray-600 hover:via-blue-600 hover:to-gray-600 text-white border border-blue-500/30 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/20 hover:shadow-blue-500/30 hover:scale-105',
    ghost: 'bg-transparent hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-300 hover:text-white border border-blue-500/30 hover:border-blue-500/50 hover:scale-105',
    danger: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-pink-600 to-red-600 hover:from-red-700 hover:via-pink-700 hover:to-red-700 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-red-500/30 hover:shadow-red-500/50 hover:scale-105',
  }

  const sizes = {
    sm: 'px-4 py-2 text-sm',
    md: 'px-6 py-2.5 text-base',
    lg: 'px-8 py-3 text-lg',
  }

  return (
    <button
      className={cn(
        'rounded-xl font-medium transition-all duration-300 backdrop-blur-sm',
        variants[variant],
        sizes[size],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  )
}

const JIKAN_API = 'https://api.jikan.moe/v4'

export interface JikanAnime {
  mal_id: number
  url: string
  images: {
    jpg: {
      image_url: string
      large_image_url: string
      small_image_url: string
    }
    webp: {
      image_url: string
      large_image_url: string
      small_image_url: string
    }
  }
  title: string
  title_english: string | null
  title_japanese: string | null
  title_synonyms: string[]
  type: string
  source: string
  episodes: number | null
  status: string
  airing: boolean
  rating: string
  score: number | null
  scored_by: number | null
  rank: number | null
  popularity: number
  members: number
  favorites: number
  synopsis: string
  background: string | null
  season: string | null
  year: number | null
  genres: Array<{
    mal_id: number
    type: string
    name: string
    url: string
  }>
}

export interface JikanSearchResponse {
  data: JikanAnime[]
  pagination: {
    last_visible_page: number
    has_next_page: boolean
    current_page: number
    items: {
      count: number
      total: number
      per_page: number
    }
  }
}

export async function searchAnime(query: string, page: number = 1, limit: number = 20): Promise<JikanSearchResponse> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${JIKAN_API}/anime?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&page=${page}&limit=${limit}&order_by=score&sort=desc&sfw=true`, {
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
    })

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Jikan API error: ${response.status}`)
    }

    const data = await response.json()
    console.log('Jikan response:', data)

    return data
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in searchAnime:', error)
    throw error
  }
}

export async function getAnimeById(id: number): Promise<JikanAnime> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${JIKAN_API}/anime/${id}/full`, {
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
    })

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Jikan API error: ${response.status}`)
    }

    const data = await response.json()
    console.log('Jikan getAnimeById response:', data)

    return data.data
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in getAnimeById:', error)
    throw error
  }
}

export async function getTopAnime(page: number = 1, limit: number = 20): Promise<JikanSearchResponse> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${JIKAN_API}/top/anime?page=${page}&limit=${limit}`, {
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
    })

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Jikan API error: ${response.status}`)
    }

    const data = await response.json()
    return data
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in getTopAnime:', error)
    throw error
  }
}

export async function getSeasonalAnime(year?: number, season?: string): Promise<JikanSearchResponse> {
  try {
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear()
    const seasons = ['winter', 'spring', 'summer', 'fall']
    const currentMonth = new Date().getMonth() + 1
    let currentSeason = seasons[Math.floor((currentMonth - 1) / 3)]

    const response = await fetch(`${JIKAN_API}/seasons/${year || currentYear}/${season || currentSeason}?limit=20`, {
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
    })

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Jikan API error: ${response.status}`)
    }

    const data = await response.json()
    return data
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in getSeasonalAnime:', error)
    throw error
  }
}

export async function getUpcomingAnime(page: number = 1, limit: number = 20): Promise<JikanSearchResponse> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${JIKAN_URL}/anime?status=upcoming&page=${page}&limit=${limit}&order_by=score&sort=desc&sfw=true`, {
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
    })

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Jikan API error: ${response.status}`)
    }

    const data = await response.json()
    return data
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in getUpcomingAnime:', error)
    throw error
  }
}

export async function getAnimeByGenre(genre: string, page: number = 1, limit: number = 20): Promise<JikanSearchResponse> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${JIKAN_API}/anime?genres=${encodeURIComponent(genre)}&page=${page}&limit=${limit}&order_by=score&sort=desc&sfw=true`, {
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
    })

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Jikan API error: ${response.status}`)
    }

    const data = await response.json()
    return data
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in getAnimeByGenre:', error)
    throw error
  }
}

export function getStatusText(status: string): string {
  const statusMap: Record<string, string> = {
    'Airing': 'Выходит',
    'Complete': 'Завершено',
    'Upcoming': 'Анонс',
    'Finished Airing': 'Завершено',
  }
  return statusMap[status] || status
}

export function getTypeText(type: string): string {
  const typeMap: Record<string, string> = {
    'TV': 'ТВ',
    'TV Short': 'ТВ (короткий)',
    'Movie': 'Фильм',
    'OVA': 'OVA',
    'ONA': 'ONA',
    'Special': 'Спец.',
    'Music': 'Музыка',
    'Unknown': 'Неизвестно',
  }
  return typeMap[type] || type
}

'use client'

import { useState, useRef } from 'react'
import { JikanAnime } from '@/lib/jikan/types'
import { GlassCard, GlassButton } from '@/components/ui/glass'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Star, Calendar, PlayCircle, Plus, Share2, Image as ImageIcon, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react'
import { AddToCollectionDialog } from './AddToCollectionDialog'
import { ShareAnimeDialog } from './ShareAnimeDialog'
import { getStatusText, getTypeText } from '@/lib/jikan/client'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'

interface JikanAnimeCardProps {
  anime: JikanAnime
}

export function JikanAnimeCard({ anime }: JikanAnimeCardProps) {
  const [isAddDialogOpen, setIsAddDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [isShareDialogOpen, setIsShareDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false)
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false)
  const cardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  const getStatusColor = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'Airing':
        return 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border-green-500/30'
      case 'Complete':
        return 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30'
      case 'Upcoming':
        return 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400 border-cyan-500/30'
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400 border-gray-500/30'
    }
  }

  const title = anime.title_english || anime.title

  const imageUrl = anime.images?.jpg?.large_image_url || anime.images?.webp?.large_image_url || ''

  return (
    <>
      <GlassCard
        ref={cardRef}
        className="overflow-hidden group cursor-pointer"
        onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}
        onMouseLeave={() => setIsHovered(false)}
      >
        <Link href={`/anime/${anime.mal_id}`}>
          <div className="relative overflow-hidden">
            {imageError || !imageUrl ? (
              <div className="w-full aspect-[2/3] bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-900/50 to-cyan-900/50 flex items-center justify-center">
                <div className="text-center p-4">
                  <ImageIcon className="h-16 w-16 mx-auto mb-3 text-blue-400/50" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-300/70">{title}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <img
                src={imageUrl}
                alt={title}
                className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-110"
                loading="lazy"
                onError={() => setImageError(true)}
              />
            )}

            {/* Overlay */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-blue-950/90 via-transparent to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300" />

            {/* Rating badge */}
            {anime.score && (
              <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-blue-950/80 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-blue-500/30 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/20">
                <Star className="h-4 w-4 fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400" />
                <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm">{anime.score.toFixed(1)}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Status badge */}
            <div className="absolute top-3 left-3">
              <Badge className={`${getStatusColor(anime.status)} border backdrop-blur-md shadow-lg`}>
                {getStatusText(anime.status)}
              </Badge>
            </div>

            {/* Rank badge */}
            {anime.rank && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 bg-blue-950/80 backdrop-blur-md px-2 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1 border border-blue-500/30">
                <TrendingUp className="h-3 w-3 text-blue-400" />
                <span className="text-white text-xs font-medium">#{anime.rank}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </Link>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
          {/* Title */}
          <Link href={`/anime/${anime.mal_id}`}>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-white line-clamp-2 hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors">
              {title}
            </h3>
          </Link>

          {/* Japanese title */}
          {anime.title_japanese && (
            <p className="text-xs text-blue-300/70 line-clamp-1">
              {anime.title_japanese}
            </p>
          )}

          {/* Genres */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {anime.genres?.slice(0, 3).map((genre) => (
              <Link key={genre.mal_id} href={`/genre/${encodeURIComponent(genre.name)}`}>
                <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs hover:bg-blue-600 hover:text-white transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30">
                  {translateGenre(genre.name)}
                </Badge>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Info */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-blue-200/70">
            {anime.episodes && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <PlayCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{anime.episodes} эп.</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {anime.year && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{anime.year}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {anime.type && (
              <Badge variant="outline" className="border-blue-500/30 text-blue-300">
                {getTypeText(anime.type)}
              </Badge>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <GlassButton
              variant="primary"
              size="sm"
              className="flex-1"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault()
                setIsAddDialogOpen(true)
              }}
            >
              <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
              В коллекцию
            </GlassButton>
            <GlassButton
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault()
                setIsShareDialogOpen(true)
              }}
            >
              <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            </GlassButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      </GlassCard>

      <AddToCollectionDialog
        isOpen={isAddDialogOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAddDialogOpen(false)}
        animeId={anime.mal_id}
        animeTitle={title}
      />

      <ShareAnimeDialog
        isOpen={isShareDialogOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsShareDialogOpen(false)}
        animeId={anime.mal_id}
        animeTitle={title}
      />
    </>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
import { JikanAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/JikanAnimeCard'
import { JikanAnime } from '@/lib/jikan/types'
import { searchAnime, getTopAnime, getAiringAnime, getUpcomingAnime, getCompletedAnime, getMovies, getAnimeByGenre } from '@/lib/jikan/client'
import { GlassPanel, GlassButton } from '@/components/ui/glass'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs'
import { Loader2, Search, Filter, Sparkles, TrendingUp, Clock, Calendar, Film, CheckCircle, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react'
import { GenreFilterDialog } from '@/components/anime/GenreFilterDialog'
import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'

type TabType = 'top' | 'airing' | 'upcoming' | 'completed' | 'movies'

export default function HomePage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('top')
  const [animeList, setAnimeList] = useState<JikanAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [selectedGenre, setSelectedGenre] = useState<string>('')
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('')
  const [isGenreDialogOpen, setIsGenreDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1)
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(false)
  const [showLoadMore, setShowLoadMore] = useState(false)

  const searchTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>()
  const observerRef = useRef<IntersectionObserver>()
  const loadMoreRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  // Load anime based on tab and filters
  const loadAnime = useCallback(async (page: number = 1, append: boolean = false) => {
    try {
      if (!append) setLoading(true)
      setError('')

      let data
      switch (activeTab) {
        case 'airing':
          data = await getAiringAnime(page, 20)
          break
        case 'upcoming':
          data = await getUpcomingAnime(page, 20)
          break
        case 'completed':
          data = await getCompletedAnime(page, 20)
          break
        case 'movies':
          data = await getMovies(page, 20)
          break
        case 'top':
        default:
          data = await getTopAnime(page, 20)
          break
      }

      const newAnime = data.data || []
      setAnimeList(prev => append ? [...prev, ...newAnime] : newAnime)
      setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
      setCurrentPage(page)
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error loading anime:', err)
      setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме. Попробуйте позже.')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [activeTab])

  // Load anime when tab changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return
    loadAnime(1, false)
  }, [activeTab, mounted])

  // Load anime by genre
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted || !selectedGenre) return

    const loadAnimeByGenre = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await getAnimeByGenre(selectedGenre, 1, 20)
        setAnimeList(data.data || [])
        setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
        setCurrentPage(1)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime by genre:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме по жанру.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnimeByGenre()
  }, [selectedGenre, mounted])

  // Debounced search
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    clearTimeout(searchTimeoutRef.current)

    if (!searchQuery.trim() || searchQuery.length < 2) {
      if (selectedGenre) {
        // Reset to genre filter if search is cleared
        return
      }
      setAnimeList([])
      setHasMore(false)
      setShowLoadMore(false)
      return
    }

    searchTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await searchAnime(searchQuery, 1, 5)
        setAnimeList(data.data || [])
        setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
        setCurrentPage(1)
        setShowLoadMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Search error:', err)
        setError('Не удалось выполнить поиск.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }, 300)

    return () => clearTimeout(searchTimeoutRef.current)
  }, [searchQuery, mounted])

  // Load more anime
  const loadMore = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loading || !hasMore) return

    try {
      setLoading(true)
      const nextPage = currentPage + 1

      let data
      if (searchQuery) {
        data = await searchAnime(searchQuery, nextPage, 20)
      } else if (selectedGenre) {
        data = await getAnimeByGenre(selectedGenre, nextPage, 20)
      } else {
        switch (activeTab) {
          case 'airing':
            data = await getAiringAnime(nextPage, 20)
            break
          case 'upcoming':
            data = await getUpcomingAnime(nextPage, 20)
            break
          case 'completed':
            data = await getCompletedAnime(nextPage, 20)
            break
          case 'movies':
            data = await getMovies(nextPage, 20)
            break
          case 'top':
          default:
            data = await getTopAnime(nextPage, 20)
            break
        }
      }

      const newAnime = data.data || []
      setAnimeList(prev => [...prev, ...newAnime])
      setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
      setCurrentPage(nextPage)
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error loading more anime:', err)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [loading, hasMore, currentPage, searchQuery, selectedGenre, activeTab])

  // Intersection Observer for infinite scroll
  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries[0].isIntersecting && hasMore && !loading) {
          loadMore()
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.1 }
    )

    if (loadMoreRef.current) {
      observer.observe(loadMoreRef.current)
    }

    return () => {
      observer.disconnect()
    }
  }, [hasMore, loading, loadMore])

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-950 via-cyan-950/20 to-blue-950">
        <div className="relative">
          <Loader2 className="h-16 w-16 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-3xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-950 via-cyan-950/20 to-blue-950">
      {/* Hero Section */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-24 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600/20 via-cyan-600/10 to-blue-600/20 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjA1KSIvPjwvc3ZnPg==')] opacity-20" />
        <div className="container mx-auto text-center relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 mb-6">
            <Sparkles className="h-10 w-10 text-blue-400 animate-pulse" />
            <h1 className="text-7xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 via-cyan-400 to-blue-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent animate-gradient-x">
              AnimeTracker
            </h1>
            <Sparkles className="h-10 w-10 text-cyan-400 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-2xl text-blue-200/80 mb-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
            Отслеживайте своё аниме, находите друзей и общайтесь — всё в одном месте
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Search and Filters */}
      <section className="px-4 mb-8">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <GlassPanel className="p-6">
            <div className="flex gap-4 mb-4">
              <div className="flex-1 relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Поиск аниме..."
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-12 bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-blue-300/50 focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-blue-500/20 h-14 text-lg"
                />
              </div>
              <GlassButton
                variant="primary"
                size="lg"
                onClick={() => setIsGenreDialogOpen(true)}
              >
                <Filter className="h-5 w-5 mr-2" />
                Жанры
              </GlassButton>
              {selectedGenre && (
                <GlassButton
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="lg"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSelectedGenre('')
                    setSearchQuery('')
                  }}
                >
                  <span className="mr-2">{translateGenre(selectedGenre)}</span>
                  ✕
                </GlassButton>
              )}
            </div>
          </GlassPanel>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Anime Tabs */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <Tabs value={activeTab} onValueChange={(v) => {
            setActiveTab(v as TabType)
            setSelectedGenre('')
            setSearchQuery('')
            setCurrentPage(1)
          }} className="w-full">
            <TabsList className="grid w-full grid-cols-5 mb-8 bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 h-16">
              <TabsTrigger value="top" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Топ
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="airing" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <Clock className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Онгоинги
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="upcoming" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <Calendar className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Анонсы
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="completed" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Завершенные
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="movies" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gradient-to-r data-[state=active]:from-blue-600 data-[state=active]:to-cyan-600 data-[state=active]:text-white data-[state=active]:border-0 h-full text-base font-medium">
                <Film className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Фильмы
              </TabsTrigger>
            </TabsList>

            {loading && animeList.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-32">
                <div className="relative">
                  <Loader2 className="h-16 w-16 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-3xl animate-pulse" />
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : error ? (
              <div className="text-center py-32">
                <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 mb-6">
                  <span className="text-5xl">⚠️</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-red-400 text-xl mb-4">{error}</p>
              </div>
            ) : animeList.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-32">
                <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 mb-6">
                  <span className="text-5xl">🔍</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-blue-300/70 text-xl">
                  {searchQuery ? 'Ничего не найдено' : 'Нет аниме в этой категории'}
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6">
                  {animeList.map((anime) => (
                    <JikanAnimeCard key={anime.mal_id} anime={anime} />
                  ))}
                </div>

                {/* Load More Button */}
                {hasMore && (
                  <div className="flex justify-center mt-12">
                    <GlassButton
                      variant="primary"
                      size="lg"
                      onClick={loadMore}
                      disabled={loading}
                      className="px-12"
                    >
                      {loading ? (
                        <>
                          <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
                          Загрузка...
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          Показать еще
                          <ChevronDown className="ml-2 h-5 w-5" />
                        </>
                      )}
                    </GlassButton>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Invisible element for intersection observer */}
                <div ref={loadMoreRef} className="h-1" />
              </>
            )}
          </Tabs>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-blue-500/20 py-8 px-4 bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl">
        <div className="container mx-auto text-center text-blue-300/70">
          <p>© 2024 AnimeTracker. Все права защищены.</p>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Genre Filter Dialog */}
      <GenreFilterDialog
        isOpen={isGenreDialogOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsGenreDialogOpen(false)}
        selectedGenre={selectedGenre}
        onGenreSelect={(genre) => {
          setSelectedGenre(genre)
          setSearchQuery('')
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import { GlassButton } from '@/components/ui/glass'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select'
import { Star, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

interface AddToCollectionDialogProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  animeId: number
  animeTitle: string
}

export function AddToCollectionDialog({ isOpen, onClose, animeId, animeTitle }: AddToCollectionDialogProps) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'watching' | 'completed' | 'plan_to_watch' | 'dropped'>('watching')
  const [rating, setRating] = useState<number>(0)
  const [review, setReview] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')

    try {
      const supabase = createClient()
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

      if (!user) {
        throw new Error('Вы не авторизованы')
      }

      const { error } = await supabase.from('anime_collection').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        anime_id: animeId,
        status,
        rating: rating > 0 ? rating : null,
        review: review.trim() || null,
      })

      if (error) throw error

      onClose()
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Ошибка при добавлении')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  if (!isOpen) return null

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {error && (
        <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="status" className="text-blue-300">Статус</Label>
        <Select value={status} onValueChange={(value: any) => setStatus(value)}>
          <SelectTrigger className="bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white">
            <SelectValue placeholder="Выберите статус" />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent className="bg-blue-950/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30">
            <SelectItem value="watching">👀 Смотрю</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="completed">✅ Просмотрено</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="plan_to_watch">📋 В планах</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="dropped">❌ Брошено</SelectItem>
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="rating" className="text-blue-300">Оценка (1-100)</Label>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Star className="h-5 w-5 text-yellow-400" />
          <Input
            id="rating"
            type="number"
            min="1"
            max="100"
            placeholder="Оцените от 1 до 100"
            value={rating || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setRating(parseInt(e.target.value) || 0)}
            className="bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="review" className="text-blue-300">Отзыв</Label>
        <Textarea
          id="review"
          placeholder="Напишите ваш отзыв..."
          value={review}
          onChange={(e) => setReview(e.target.value)}
          rows={4}
          className="bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-blue-300/50"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
        <GlassButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={onClose}
          disabled={loading}
          className="flex-1"
        >
          Отмена
        </GlassButton>
        <GlassButton
          variant="primary"
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={loading}
          className="flex-1"
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
              Добавление...
            </>
          ) : (
            'Добавить'
          )}
        </GlassButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// Жанры Jikan API
const JIKAN_GENRES = [

interface GenreFilterDialogProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  selectedGenre: string
  onGenreSelect: (genre: string) => void
}

export function GenreFilterDialog({ isOpen, onClose, selectedGenre, onGenreSelect }: GenreFilterDialogProps) {

  if (!isOpen) return null

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="bg-blue-950/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 max-w-3xl max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <Filter className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
            Фильтр по жанрам
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="text-blue-300/70">
            Выберите жанр для фильтрации аниме
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 py-4">
          {JIKAN_GENRES.map((genre) => (
            <Badge
              key={genre}
              variant={selectedGenre === genre ? 'default' : 'secondary'}
              className={`cursor-pointer transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105 ${
                selectedGenre === genre
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 text-white border-0 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30'
                  : 'bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/50'
              }`}
              onClick={() => {
                onGenreSelect(selectedGenre === genre ? '' : genre)
                onClose()
              }}
            >
              {translateGenre(genre)}
            </Badge>
          ))}
        </div>

        {selectedGenre && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-blue-500/20">
            <span className="text-sm text-blue-300">
              Выбран: <span className="text-white font-medium">{translateGenre(selectedGenre)}</span>
            </span>
            <GlassButton
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => {
                onGenreSelect('')
                onClose()
              }}
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Сбросить
            </GlassButton>
          </div>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { GlassButton } from '@/components/ui/glass'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Share2, Copy, Check, MessageCircle, Globe } from 'lucide-react'

interface ShareAnimeDialogProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  animeId: number
  animeTitle: string
}

export function ShareAnimeDialog({ isOpen, onClose, animeId, animeTitle }: ShareAnimeDialogProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/anime/${animeId}`
  const shareText = `Посмотри это аниме: ${animeTitle}`

  const copyToClipboard = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl)
      setCopied(true)
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to copy:', error)
    }
  }

  const shareOnTwitter = () => {
    const url = `https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${encodeURIComponent(shareText)}&url=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }

  const shareOnFacebook = () => {
    const url = `https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }

  const shareNative = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title: animeTitle,
          text: shareText,
          url: shareUrl,
        })
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error sharing:', error)
      }
    }
  }

  if (!isOpen) return null

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="bg-blue-950/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/20 max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="text-white">Поделиться аниме</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="text-blue-300/70">
            Поделись ссылкой на {animeTitle} с друзьями
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={shareUrl}
                readOnly
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-blue-950/50 border border-blue-500/30 text-white rounded-md"
              />
            </div>
            <GlassButton
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              onClick={copyToClipboard}
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />}
            </GlassButton>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <GlassButton
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={shareNative}
            >
              <Share2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Поделиться
            </GlassButton>
            <GlassButton
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={shareOnTwitter}
            >
              <MessageCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Twitter
            </GlassButton>
            <GlassButton
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={shareOnFacebook}
            >
              <Globe className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Facebook
            </GlassButton>
            <GlassButton
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={copyToClipboard}
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />}
              {copied ? 'Скопировано!' : 'Копировать'}
            </GlassButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

      } finally {
        setLoading(false)

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { JikanAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/JikanAnimeCard'
import { JikanAnime } from '@/lib/jikan/types'
import { getAnimeByGenre } from '@/lib/jikan/client'
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft, Filter } from 'lucide-react'
import { translateGenre } from '@/lib/genres'
import Link from 'next/link'

export default function GenrePage() {
  const params = useParams()
  const genre = decodeURIComponent(params.genre as string)
  const [animeList, setAnimeList] = useState<JikanAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1)
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    const loadAnime = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await getAnimeByGenre(genre, 1, 20)
        setAnimeList(data.data || [])
        setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
        setCurrentPage(1)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error loading anime by genre:', err)
        setError('Не удалось загрузить аниме по жанру. Попробуйте позже.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }

    loadAnime()
  }, [genre, mounted])

  const loadMore = async () => {
    if (loading || !hasMore) return

    try {
      setLoading(true)
      const nextPage = currentPage + 1
      const data = await getAnimeByGenre(genre, nextPage, 20)
      setAnimeList(prev => [...prev, ...(data.data || [])])
      setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)

      console.error('Error loading more anime:', err)

      setLoading(false)

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-3xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>

Теперь создам страницу поиска с миллиардным дизайном:

Write c:\Users\denis\Desktop\Claude\src\app\search\page.tsx
225 lines
'use client'

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
import { JikanAnimeCard } from '@/components/anime/JikanAnimeCard'
import { JikanAnime } from '@/lib/jikan/types'
import { searchAnime } from '@/lib/jikan/client'
import { GlassPanel, GlassButton } from '@/components/ui/glass'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Loader2, Search, Sparkles, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SearchPage() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [results, setResults] = useState<JikanAnime[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1)
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(false)
  const [showLoadMore, setShowLoadMore] = useState(false)

  const searchTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>()
  const loadMoreRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  // Debounced search
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return

    clearTimeout(searchTimeoutRef.current)

    if (!query.trim() || query.length < 2) {
      setResults([])
      setHasMore(false)
      setShowLoadMore(false)
      return
    }

    searchTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true)
        setError('')

        const data = await searchAnime(query, 1, 5)
        setResults(data.data || [])
        setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
        setCurrentPage(1)
        setShowLoadMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Search error:', err)
        setError('Не удалось выполнить поиск. Попробуйте позже.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    }, 300)

    return () => clearTimeout(searchTimeoutRef.current)
  }, [query, mounted])

  // Load more results
  const loadMore = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loading || !hasMore) return

    try {
      setLoading(true)
      const nextPage = currentPage + 1
      const data = await searchAnime(query, nextPage, 20)
      setResults(prev => [...prev, ...(data.data || [])])
      setHasMore(data.pagination?.has_next_page || false)
      setCurrentPage(nextPage)
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error loading more results:', err)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [loading, hasMore, currentPage, query])

  // Intersection Observer for infinite scroll
  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries[0].isIntersecting && hasMore && !loading) {
          loadMore()
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.1 }
    )

    if (loadMoreRef.current) {
      observer.observe(loadMoreRef.current)
    }

    return () => {
      observer.disconnect()
    }
  }, [hasMore, loading, loadMore])

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-950 via-cyan-950/20 to-blue-950">
        <div className="relative">
          <Loader2 className="h-16 w-16 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-3xl animate-pulse" />
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-950 via-cyan-950/20 to-blue-950">
      {/* Header */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-20 px-4">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600/30 via-cyan-600/20 to-blue-600/30 animate-gradient-x" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iNDAiIGhlaWdodD0iNDAiIHhtbG5zPSJodHRwOi8vd3d3LnczLm9yZy8yMDAwL3N2ZyI+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iMjAiIGN5PSIyMCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjA1KSIvPjwvc3ZnPg==')] opacity-20" />
        <div className="container mx-auto text-center relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mb-6">
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-blue-400 animate-pulse" />
            <h1 className="text-5xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-cyan-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Поиск аниме
            </h1>
            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-cyan-400 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xl text-blue-200/80 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Найдите аниме и добавьте в свою коллекцию
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Search */}
      <section className="px-4 pb-16">
        <div className="container mx-auto">
          <GlassPanel className="p-6 mb-8">
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="flex-1 relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Введите название аниме..."
                  value={query}
                  onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="pl-12 bg-blue-950/50 backdrop-blur-xl border-blue-500/30 text-white placeholder:text-blue-300/50 focus:border-blue-500/50 focus:ring-blue-500/20 h-14 text-lg"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </GlassPanel>

          {error && (
            <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
              <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-6 py-4 rounded-xl text-center">
                {error}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {loading && results.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-32">
              <div className="relative">
                <Loader2 className="h-16 w-16 animate-spin text-blue-500" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/20 blur-3xl animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : results.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6">
                {results.map((anime) => (
                  <JikanAnimeCard key={anime.mal_id} anime={anime} />
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Load More Button */}
              {hasMore && (
                <div className="flex justify-center mt-12">
                  <GlassButton
                    variant="primary"
                    size="lg"
                    onClick={loadMore}
                    disabled={loading}
                    className="px-12"
                  >
                    {loading ? (
                      <>
                        <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
                        Загрузка...
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        Показать еще
                        <ChevronDown className="ml-2 h-5 w-5" />
                      </>
                    )}
                  </GlassButton>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Invisible element for intersection observer */}
              <div ref={loadMoreRef} className="h-1" />
            </>
          ) : query && query.length >= 2 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-32">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 mb-6">
                <span className="text-5xl">🔍</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-blue-300/70 text-xl">
                Ничего не найдено. Попробуйте другой запрос.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-center py-32">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 mb-6">
                <Search className="h-10 w-10 text-blue-400" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-blue-300/70 text-xl">
                Введите название аниме для поиска
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

✅ Что было сделано:
1. Glassmorphism компоненты - созданы крутые стеклянные панели:
GlassCard - карточки с glassmorphism эффектом
GlassPanel - панели с glassmorphism эффектом
GlassButton - кнопки с glassmorphism эффектом
2. Jikan API - полностью переключился на Jikan API вместо AniList:
searchAnime() - поиск с пагинацией
getAnimeById() - получение аниме по ID
getTopAnime() - топ аниме
getAiringAnime() - онгоинги
getUpcomingAnime() - анонсы
getCompletedAnime() - завершенные
getMovies() - фильмы
getAnimeByGenre() - по жанру
3. Умный поиск - с debounce и пагинацией:
Debounce 300ms для быстрого поиска
Показывает 5 результатов сразу
Кнопка "Показать еще" для загрузки еще 20
Infinite scroll с Intersection Observer
4. Миллиардный дизайн - на всех страницах:
Главная страница: крутой hero section, glassmorphism панели, вкладки с иконками
Страница аниме: красивый баннер, статистика в карточках, glassmorphism эффекты
Страница жанра: красивый заголовок, адаптивная сетка
Страница поиска: большой поиск, glassmorphism панель, пагинация
5. Карточки аниме - с крутым дизайном:
Glassmorphism эффект
Hover анимации с scale
Градиентные тени
Статус с цветовой индикацией
Рейтинг и ранг
Кликабельные жанры
6. Темно-синяя тема:
Очень темно-синий фон (oklch(0.06 0.01 240))
Фиолетово-синие акценты
Glassmorphism эффекты с blur
Кастомный скроллбар
7. Оптимизация производительности:
GPU ускорение для анимаций
Smooth scrolling
Оптимизированные переходы
Debounce для поиска
Intersection Observer для infinite scroll
Image rendering optimization
8. Функциональность:
